                def run(event_name, *args)
          
      157.

                  hooks = @registry[event_name].sort_by { |h| h[:priority] }

                  hooks.each do |hook|

                    result = execute_hook(hook[:handler], *args)

      163. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    163. 
            

            
              
            

                  rescue StandardError => e

                    handle_hook_error(e, event_name, hook)

      177.

                  hooks = @registry[event_name].sort_by { |h| h[:priority] }
          
      178.
